- [Phase 12]: [12-06]: GATE-03's audited set is DECLARED LITERALLY in `src/lib/design/live-regions.ts` — nine booker-path files as a const tuple, ten Phase-13 surfaces plus Phase 14's `address-autocomplete.tsx` as a sibling `LIVE_REGION_EXCLUSIONS` const with a stated owner per row, and thirteen regions in a TOTAL `Record<LiveRegionId, LiveRegionRow>` carrying `{ file, kind, at, announces, why }`. `DeclaredFileCountIsNine` pins the set's size under `tsc --noEmit` and its ALIAS NAME carries the number, so 12-12 (`relax-band.tsx`) and 12-13 (`collision-notice.tsx`) must rename it as they widen. **12-UI-SPEC's falsifiable claim #3 — "every `role="status"` on that set resolves to a non-empty accessible name" — is FALSE as a blanket universal of the tree the same document says to KEEP**, and the resolution is recorded rather than absorbed: `status` is `nameFrom: author`, so six shipped content-bearing regions (`reserve-actions`, `book-cta`, `spots-left-chip`, `hold-expired-state`, both `slot-picker` regions) compute `""` CORRECTLY — their text IS the message, and an `aria-label` on such a region can be announced *instead of* its content on VoiceOver/Safari. The checkable property is narrower: **a live region whose content is decorative by construction must be author-named**, which is exactly `kind: "loading"`. So `kind` DECIDES the naming mechanism (no per-row `nameFrom` field a row could set to whatever passes), `aria-busy="true"` classifies as `loading` FIRST so a skeleton cannot be relabelled to dodge the label, and the gate asserts the mapping in both directions. **A fifth kind, `threshold`, exists because a role would break the property GATE-03 is named for**: `hold-countdown.tsx`'s sr-only span drops its `aria-live` on expiry while keeping its text, and `role="status"` is a PERMANENT implicit polite region — adding one would make `HoldExpiredState` a second region announcing one event (rule 6) and would break 12-03's measured `toBe(1)`. Exactly one such region is asserted. Rows key to elements as `{file}#{kind}#{at}` where `at` is the ordinal WITHIN a kind (per-kind, not per-file, so a new `alert` does not renumber two `status` rows) — and probe (b) measured the residual: an INSERTION among same-kind siblings takes the displaced row's identity, so the failure message now prints the whole file's source-order sequence. Four regions corrected: `hold-expired-state.tsx` drops the interrupting politeness level (rule 7; the shipped focus move to the primary CTA is what earns it), `availability-calendar.tsx` + `date-pass-picker.tsx` loading regions gain `role="status"` + `aria-label` + an `sr-only` child from ONE binding with every bar `aria-hidden` (rules 4+5), `slot-picker.tsx`'s pending helper gains `role="status"` (rule 5). `grep -rn 'aria-live="assertive"' src/components/` now returns NOTHING at all. GATE-03 marked complete: the four `role="alert"` / content-region KEEP decisions are recorded as rows rather than edits, and the compile+scan gate forces 12-12/12-13 to declare their own.

> verification `passed`): `claimCheckoutLease` is a CAS checkout lease proven over three independent
> Postgres connections at both the inlined-pattern and shipped-production layers, with four mutations
> recorded RED — so two concurrent claims for one `holdId` can never both reach PayMongo. (The underlying
> finding stands and is worth remembering: **PayMongo does not honour `Idempotency-Key` on

> not the header, is the guard.) CR-02's live re-price assertion was closed earlier, on 2026-07-29 by

~~🔴 **OPEN BLOCKER — `confirmBooking` double-charges (found 2026-07-28 by the 08-17 human UAT; `deferred-items.md` item 5).**~~ **CLOSED 2026-08-01 by `260801-kv2`.** A booker who submits **Confirm & pay** twice is charged twice: measured live, ₱1,470.00 collected against a ₱735.00 booking (`pay_YFn1jgMWyau46ECydfPR2LdN` + `pay_iw2zsj3ehcRzgRYzgn7hkoUP`, booking `FIT-JSHBVKFP`), both `qrph` and therefore **unrefundable via the API**. Root cause: **PayMongo does not honor `Idempotency-Key` on `/v1/checkout_sessions`** (probed: identical key + body → two different session ids), while three shipped comments assert it does (`booking.ts:582`, `booking.ts:592`, `paymongo.ts:172`). **Not a CR-02 regression and wider than CR-02 — flat-priced bookings are equally exposed and this predates Phase 8.** Fix: `confirmBooking` must expire the persisted `checkout_session_id` before creating a new one, mirroring `updateDeclaredPax`, **with a test that drives the real PayMongo API rather than a mock.** Route via `/gsd-plan-phase 8 --gaps`.

~~🟡 **OPEN — CR-02's live re-price assertion has never been exercised by a human** (`deferred-items.md` item 6). 08-17 step 4 ran two submissions at the same headcount, so `updateDeclaredPax` was never entered. Its only evidence is 08-13's mock-backed mutation proofs.~~ **CLOSED 2026-07-29 by `08-19` case 3 (`54ffc6f`), on the real `sk_test_` API.**

Open product decisions to resolve before their relevant phase begins (from research):

- ✅ RESOLVED (Phase 3, D-22): Slot granularity = 60-min on-the-hour, platform-wide for v1; any run of consecutive hours or a full day (D-23). Per-listing granularity deferred.
- ✅ RESOLVED (Phase 6 discuss, D-63/D-64, 2026-07-19): Request-to-book money model = **pay-on-approval** (no charge at request; the ~7-day card auth-hold limit is moot — no hold). Approval SLA = 24h, post-approval payment window = 24h, both config-tunable. QRPh is back IN scope for request-to-book (nothing ever reverses).
- ✅ RESOLVED (Phase 7): Cancellation/refund policy matrix → D-67/D-68 tier ladder (07-03, shipped through 07-15). The QRPh refund rule is now SETTLED BY OBSERVATION (07-16, 2026-07-23): QRPh is NOT API-refundable at all (HTTP 400, verbatim in refund-rail.ts) — the D-72 collect-and-never-store InstaPay path is built for it.
- ✅ RESOLVED (2026-07-25): Phase 8 payment-model gate — **per-attendee cost-splitting IS on FitOut's roadmap** (user product decision). v1 group bookings stay a free RSVP coordination shell (RSVP never touches occupancy, never per-attendee payment), but that shell is now a JUSTIFIED FOUNDATION for the future paid/cost-split version, not standalone busywork. GPAY-01 (cost-splitting) / GPAY-02 (ticketing) remain OUT of v1 but are a future milestone, not "never." Unblocks 08-CONTEXT.md.
- Phase 8 (still open, for the discussion): Whether invited attendees need an account to RSVP (v1 default: tokenized link, no account); group capacity source (listing capacity vs per-booking limit) — the 4 gray areas to work in /gsd-discuss-phase 8.
- Phase 2 / Phase 5: Cold-start liquidity — consider lightweight admin/seed tooling and a zero-result-search metric; do not over-build.
- ✅ RESOLVED (Phase 2): WR-06 (rate-limit + audit on capability-activate/onboarding actions) CLOSED in Plan 02. WR-04 (email-send retry/observability) STILL OPEN — deferred to the Phase-7 transactional-email layer.
- ✅ RESOLVED (2026-07-10): 02-VALIDATION.md regenerated for PayMongo (tests/paymongo/*, merchant.activated, Paymongo-Signature) and marked Nyquist-compliant; the flaky public-listing E2E seed was fixed. 02-RESEARCH/02-PATTERNS remain Stripe-era historical (non-blocking).

📌 **Two contracts this planning round establishes, load-bearing for execution:**

1. *The four UNVERIFIED findings (CR-02, CR-04, CR-05, CR-06) are planned CONFIRM-THEN-FIX, and the not-reproduced branch is a real branch.* Each plan's Task 1 must reproduce the defect against unchanged `src/` and record it verbatim; if it does NOT reproduce, the executor writes a dated `CR-NN — NOT REPRODUCED (false positive)` entry in `deferred-items.md`, KEEPS the test as a regression guard, changes nothing in `src/`, and closes the plan. `git status --short -- src/` must be empty at the end of every Task 1. The reviewer was right 2-for-2 on what was checked — that is not a guarantee for the other four, and a "fix" for a defect that does not exist is worse than doing nothing.
2. *CR-01's test is the whole lesson of this phase, encoded.* CR-01 survived 1035 green tests, a 21/21 browser proof AND a 9/9 human UAT because **both proof layers were structurally blind to the same-day path** (the e2e spec books only `offset >= 3` days out; the walkthrough ran at 01:31 Makati, before the 06:00 opening). 09-17's fixture is therefore **deterministic at any run hour** (`open_time` = venue-local now − 2h floored, never before `00:00`; `close_time` = `23:59`) — a test that only catches the bug when the suite happens to run during business hours reproduces the exact failure being fixed. The mutation's RED message is named VERBATIM and the assertion is ORDERED FIRST so it quotes the sentence a booker would read.

⚠️ **The unsatisfiable-acceptance-grep defect was fixed at the SOURCE this round, not absorbed a 20th time.** The original 16 plans shipped **19** of them. The checker caught the 20th in 09-25: Task 2 demanded both a clean `npm run lint` and a LITERAL-apostrophe grep, but `react/no-unescaped-entities` forces `can&apos;t` in JSX text — the two criteria were mutually unsatisfiable. Fixed by prescribing an exported `PASS_NON_REFUNDABLE_MESSAGE` constant rendered as `{PASS_NON_REFUNDABLE_MESSAGE}` (inside a TS string literal the apostrophe needs no escaping, so the rule never fires and the source bytes and the grep pattern are the same). A companion criterion was also **vacuous** — it grepped a literal apostrophe in text that really contains `&apos;`, so "UNCHANGED" held no matter what happened to the paragraph. **Both fixes carry an inline note explaining why, so the next reader cannot "fix" them back.**

⚠️ **`src/lib/availability/units.ts` is edited by FOUR gap plans in sequence — 09-18 → 09-20 → 09-21 → 09-23 — all extending the SAME step-5 refusal-branch sequence in `createOpenCapacityHold`.** Each plan's `read_first` now names which predecessors changed it and what they added, and 09-23 is instructed to leave 09-20's block refusal and 09-21's rate refusal alone. This was the checker's third warning and it is the seam most likely to produce a merge-shaped bug during execution.
